Method: 提出UnIte方法，包含两个步骤：(1) 过滤具有高偶然不确定性(aleatoric uncertainty)的文档；(2) 优先选择具有高认知不确定性(epistemic uncertainty)的文档进行迭代采样，以最大化当前模型的学习效用。

Result: 在BEIR基准上使用小模型和大模型进行大量实验，平均仅用4k训练样本，分别取得+2.45和+3.49 nDCG@10的显著提升。

Conclusion: 基于不确定性的文档采样策略能有效提升无监督域适应中神经检索器的泛化性能，且所需训练样本更少。

Abstract: Unsupervised domain adaptation generalizes neural retrievers to an unseen domain by generating pseudo queries on target domain documents. The quality and efficiency of this adaptation critically depend on which documents are selected for pseudo query generation. The existing document sampling method focuses on diversity but fails to capture model uncertainty. In contrast, we propose **Un**certainty-based **Ite**rative Document Sampling (UnIte) addressing these limitations by (1) filtering documents with high aleatoric uncertainty and (2) prioritizing those with high epistemic uncertainty, maximizing the learning utility of the current model. We conducted extensive experiments on a large corpus of BEIR with small and large models, showing significant gains of +2.45 and +3.49 nDCG@10 with a smaller training sample size, 4k on average.

</details>


### [112] [From Local Indices to Global Identifiers: Generative Reranking for Recommender Systems via Global Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25291)
*Pengyue Jia,Xiaobei Wang,Yingyi Zhang,Shuchang Liu,Yupeng Hou,Hailan Yang,Xu Gao,Xiaopeng Li,Yejing Wang,Julian McAuley,Xiang Li,Lantao Hu,Yongqi Liu,Kaiqiao Zhan,Han Li,Kun Gai,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了GloRank，一种将列表重排序从局部索引选择转变为全局标识符生成的生成式框架，通过离散token表示和两阶段优化（监督预训练+强化学习后训练），在公开数据集和工业数据集上均取得了优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有列表重排序方法将任务建模为从局部输入列表中选择索引，导致语义不一致的动作空间：同一输出神经元在不同样本中代表不同物品，模型无法建立稳定、本质的物品理解。

Method: 1) 将物品表示为离散token序列，将重排序重构为token生成任务；2) 解耦评分机制与可变输入顺序，使物品在一致的全局标准下被评估；3) 两阶段优化：监督预训练初始化模型，强化学习后训练直接最大化列表级效用。

Result: 在两个公开基准数据集、一个大规模工业数据集以及在线A/B测试中，GloRank持续优于最先进的基线方法，并在冷启动场景中展现出卓越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过将动作空间从局部索引切换到全局标识符的生成式框架，有效解决了语义不一致问题，提升了重排序阶段的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In modern recommender systems, list-wise reranking serves as a critical phase within the multi-stage pipeline, finalizing the exposed item sequence and directly impacting user satisfaction by modeling complex intra-list item dependencies. Existing methods typically formulate this task as selecting indices from the local input list. However, this approach suffers from a semantically inconsistent action space: the same output neuron (logits) represents different items across different samples, preventing the model from establishing a stable, intrinsic understanding of the items. To address this, we propose GloRank (Global Action Space Ranker), a generative framework that shifts reranking from selecting local indices to generating global identifiers. Specifically, we represent items as sequences of discrete tokens and reformulate reranking as a token generation task. This design effectively decouples the scoring mechanism from the variable input order, ensuring that items are evaluated against a consistent global standard. We further enhance this with a two-stage optimization pipeline: a supervised pre-training phase to initialize the model with high-quality demonstrations, followed by a reinforcement learning-based post-training phase to directly maximize list-wise utility. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks and a large-scale industrial dataset, coupled with online A/B tests, demonstrate that GloRank consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and achieves superior robustness in cold-start scenarios.

</details>


### [113] [Stop Using the Wilcoxon Test: Myth, Misconception and Misuse in IR Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25349)
*Julián Urbano*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出Wilcoxon符号秩检验在IR评估中被长期误用，该检验并非t检验的合理非参数替代，反而容易导致Type I错误率失控，建议弃用Wilcoxon并改用更稳健的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IR领域普遍认为Wilcoxon检验因其非参数性质而比t检验更安全，但这种认知源于统计学教材对检验假设的模糊和不一致表述，导致该检验被误用数十年。

Method: 通过系统性文献综述、理论分析以及基于TREC数据的实证演示，揭示Wilcoxon检验在IR设定下Type I错误率失控的机制。

Result: Wilcoxon检验在IR评估中容易失去对Type I错误率的控制，其被当作安全替代方案的认知具有误导性，反而引入了更严重的问题。

Conclusion: 在IR评估中继续使用Wilcoxon检验是不合理的，放弃使用该检验将提升领域方法论的严谨性。

Abstract: In benchmarking of Information Retrieval systems, the Wilcoxon signed-rank test is often treated as a safer alternative to the t-test. This belief is fueled by textbooks and recommendations that portray Wilcoxon as the proper non-parametric alternative because metric scores are not normally distributed. We argue that this narrative is misleading and harmful. A careful review of Statistics textbooks reveals inconsistencies and omissions in how the assumptions underlying these tests are presented, fostering confusion that has propagated into IR research. As a result, Wilcoxon has been routinely misapplied for decades, creating a false sense of safety against a threat that was never there to begin with, while introducing another one so severe that it virtually guarantees the test will break down and mislead researchers. Through a combination of systematic literature review, analysis and empirical demonstrations with TREC data, we show how and why the Wilcoxon test easily loses control of its Type I error rate in IR settings. We conclude that the continued use of Wilcoxon in IR evaluation is unjustified and that abandoning it would improve the methodological soundness of our field.

</details>


### [114] [The Attention Market: Interpreting Online Fair Re-ranking as Manifold Optimization under Walrasian Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25577)
*Chen Xu,Wei Chu,Wenyu Hu,Fengran Mo,Jun Xu,Maarten de Rijke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ManifoldRank，一种基于瓦尔拉斯均衡和流形优化的在线公平重排序算法，通过将公平性视为税收成本并在供需两侧进行梯度调整，有效平衡了公平性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线公平重排序方法在20种不同设置下表现差异显著，缺乏理论基础解释其不一致性。

Method: 将公平重排序重构为注意力市场框架下的瓦尔拉斯均衡问题，公平性作为税收成本；结合流形优化，证明寻找均衡等价于在特定排序流形上进行梯度下降；提出ManifoldRank算法，供应侧根据公平需求调整梯度（考虑成本），需求侧根据排序分数预测额外梯度调整项，二者整合以平衡公平与准确。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验验证了ManifoldRank的有效性。

Conclusion: 不同重排序设置对应不同流形几何，这些几何差异决定了梯度景观和优化轨迹；ManifoldRank通过对齐排序流形的梯度调整，在多种上下文中有效实现公平性保障。

Abstract: Fair re-ranking aims to promote long-tail items and enhance diversity within groups in information retrieval. While previous research on online fairness-aware re-ranking has shown promising outcomes, our comprehensive evaluation of online fair re-ranking methods over 20 settings reveals significant performance disparities among existing methods. To uncover the root causes of these inconsistencies, we reformulate fair re-ranking within an attentional market framework governed by a Walrasian Equilibrium, where the fairness is treated as a taxation cost. This market-based formulation is then coupled with manifold optimization, demonstrating that seeking this equilibrium is equivalent to performing gradient descent on a specific ranking manifold constructed by the market. Different re-ranking settings induce distinct manifold geometries, and these intrinsic geometric differences dictate the gradient landscapes and optimization trajectories. We propose ManifoldRank, an efficient online fair re-ranking algorithm. ManifoldRank adjusts gradients to align with the ranking manifold, considering various contextual settings. On the supply side, it incorporates a gradient adjustment based on different fairness requirements, accounting for associated costs. On the demand side, it empirically predicts an additional gradient adjustment term derived from the ranking scores. By integrating these two gradient adjustments, ManifoldRank effectively balances fairness and accuracy. Experimental results across multiple datasets confirm ManifoldRank's effectiveness.

</details>


### [115] [Health System Scale Semantic Search Across Unstructured Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25605)
*Faith Wavinya Mutinda,Spandana Makeneni,Anna Lin,Shivaji Dutta,Irit R. Rasooly,Patrick Dibussolo,Shivani Kamath Belman,Hessam Shahriari,Kevin Murphy,Alex B. Ruan,Barbara H. Chaiyachati,Sanjay Chainani,Robert W. Grundmeier,Scott M. Haag,Jeffrey M. Miller,Heather M. Griffis,Ian M. Campbell*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文报告在大型儿童医院成功部署了覆盖1.66亿份临床笔记（4.84亿向量）的语义搜索系统，使用Qwen3嵌入模型和存储优化索引，实现亚秒级查询延迟（中位237ms），月成本约4,000美元，在临床问答基准上达到94.6%准确率，并将图表审查效率提升24-89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义搜索相比关键词匹配在临床信息检索上有显著优势，但将其部署到涵盖数亿临床笔记的整个医疗系统面临工程、成本和治理方面的巨大挑战，导致此前无法大规模应用。

Method: 使用instruction-tuned Qwen3-embedding-0.6B嵌入模型，将向量存储在托管数据库中并采用存储优化索引，全文本元数据保存在低延迟键值存储中，在HIPAA合规框架下运行。通过三项实验评估：使用医生撰写的基准数据集优化嵌入模型和分块策略；表征全规模性能（成本、延迟、检索质量）；通过三项任务的图表审查效率对比评估临床效用。

Result: 系统实现亚秒级查询延迟（单用户中位237ms，20用户并发中位451ms），月成本约4,000美元。Qwen3嵌入配合300词块大小在临床问答基准上达到94.6%准确率。在三项抽象任务中，语义搜索相比临床医生的手动图表审查将完成时间减少24-89%，同时保持可比的一致性。

Conclusion: 医疗系统规模的语义搜索在技术和操作层面都是可行的。该系统为交互式搜索、队列生成和下游LLM驱动的临床应用提供了基础设施，无需专门的信息学专业知识。

Abstract: Introduction: Semantic search, which retrieves documents based on conceptual similarity rather than keyword matching, offers substantial advantages for retrieval of clinical information. However, deploying semantic search across entire health systems, comprising hundreds of millions of clinical notes, presents formidable engineering, cost, and governance challenges that have prevented adoption. Methods: We deployed a semantic search system at a large children's hospital indexing 166 million clinical notes (484 million vectors) from 1.68 million patients. The system uses instruction-tuned qwen3-embedding-0.6B embeddings, stores vectors in a managed database with storage-optimized indexing, maintains full-text metadata in a low-latency key-value store, and operates within a HIPAA-compliant governance framework. We evaluated the system through three experiments: optimization of embedding model and chunking strategy using a physician-authored benchmark dataset, characterization of full-scale performance (cost, latency, retrieval quality), and clinical utility assessment via comparison of chart abstraction efficiency across three tasks. Results: The system delivers sub-second query latency (median 237 ms single-user, 451 ms 20-user concurrency) with monthly costs of approximately USD 4,000. Qwen3 embeddings with 300-token chunk size achieved 94.6% accuracy on a clinical question-answering benchmark. In clinical utility evaluation across three abstraction tasks, semantic search reduced time-to-completion by 24 to 89% compared to clinician-performed chart review while maintaining comparable inter-rater agreement. Conclusion: Health-system-scale semantic search is both technically and operationally feasible. The system provides infrastructure supporting interactive search, cohort generation, and downstream LLM-powered clinical applications without requiring specialized informatics expertise.

</details>


### [116] [K-CARE: Knowledge-driven Symmetrical Contextual Anchoring and Analogical Prototype Reasoning for E-commerce Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25683)
*Chen Yifei,Tian Zhixing,Wang Chenyang,Cheng Ziguang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对电商搜索相关性中的知识边界瓶颈，提出K-CARE框架，通过对称上下文锚定（SCA）和类比原型推理（APR）两种机制，将外部知识融入大语言模型推理过程，显著提升复杂场景下的搜索相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在电商搜索相关性中面临性能瓶颈，主要问题并非推理路径优化不足，而是模型参数化记忆缺乏领域专属知识导致的知识边界问题，这种上下文真空无法通过单纯优化推理轨迹解决。

Method: 提出K-CARE框架，包含两个协同组件：(1) 对称上下文锚定（SCA），利用行为衍生的隐式知识锚定查询和商品以填充上下文真空；(2) 类比原型推理（APR），利用专家策划的原型知识通过上下文类比来校准决策边界。

Result: 在主流电商平台上的大量离线评估和在线A/B测试中，K-CARE显著优于最先进的基线方法，通过解决知识密集型相关性挑战带来了实质性商业影响。

Conclusion: 知识边界而非推理轨迹优化是LLM在电商搜索相关性中的主要瓶颈，通过外部知识锚定和原型类比推理可有效弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: This paper targets e-commerce search relevance. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in this field, they often encounter performance bottlenecks in persistent 'corner cases' within complex industrial scenarios. Existing research primarily focuses on optimizing reasoning trajectories via Reinforcement Learning. However, real-world observations suggest that the primary bottleneck stems from knowledge boundaries, where the absence of domain-specific intelligence in the model's parametric memory creates a contextual void. This void persists when interpreting idiosyncratic queries or niche products and cannot be resolved solely through reasoning-path optimization.
  To bridge this gap, we propose K-CARE, a framework that extends the model's cognitive reach by grounding reasoning in external knowledge. K-CARE comprises two synergistic components: (1) Symmetrical Contextual Anchoring (SCA), which fills the contextual void by anchoring queries and products with behavior-derived implicit knowledge; and (2) Analogical Prototype Reasoning (APR), which leverages expert-curated prototypical knowledge to calibrate decision boundaries through in-context analogy. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests on a leading e-commerce platform demonstrate that K-CARE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, delivering substantial commercial impact by resolving knowledge-intensive relevance challenges.

</details>


### [117] [From Citation Selection to Citation Absorption: A Measurement Framework for Generative Engine Optimization Across AI Search Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25707)
*Zhang Kai,Yao Jingang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式搜索引擎优化的两阶段测量框架（引用选择与引用吸收），通过对ChatGPT、Google AI Overview/Gemini和Perplexity三个平台的602个提示词及大量数据进行分析，发现引用广度与深度存在差异：Perplexity和Google引用来源更多，但ChatGPT引用的页面影响更高；高影响力页面通常更长、结构更完整、语义对齐且富含可提取证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索引擎已从简单的信息检索演变为主动生成答案的系统，这使得在线信息能否被引用、引用后能否被吸收为答案内容成为一个关键但未被充分研究的问题。现有GEO研究多聚焦于引用数量，忽略了被引用内容在最终答案中的实际吸收程度。

Method: 提出两阶段测量框架：①引用选择（平台触发搜索并选择来源）；②引用吸收（被引用页面为最终答案贡献语言、证据、结构或事实支持）。基于geo-citation-lab公开数据集，分析ChatGPT、Google AI Overview/Gemini和Perplexity三个平台，涵盖602个受控提示词、21,143个有效搜索层引用、23,745个引用级特征记录、18,151个成功获取页面及72个提取特征。

Result: 引用广度与引用深度出现分化：Perplexity和Google平均引用更多来源，ChatGPT引用来源更少但被引用页面的平均影响力显著更高。高影响力页面通常具有更长篇幅、更完整的结构、更高的语义对齐度以及更丰富的可提取证据（如定义、数值事实、比较、程序步骤等）。

Conclusion: GEO测量不应仅停留在引用数量层面，而应将答案级别的吸收（absorption）作为一个独立的衡量维度。这为生成式搜索引擎优化提供了更精细化的评估框架。

Abstract: Generative search engines increasingly determine whether online information is merely discoverable, cited as a source, or actually absorbed into generated answers. This paper proposes a two-stage measurement framework for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO): citation selection, where a platform triggers search and chooses sources, and citation absorption, where a cited page contributes language, evidence, structure, or factual support to the final answer. We analyze the public geo-citation-lab dataset covering 602 controlled prompts across ChatGPT, Google AI Overview/Gemini, and Perplexity; 21,143 valid search-layer citations; 23,745 citation-level feature records; 18,151 successfully fetched pages; and 72 extracted features. The central descriptive finding is that citation breadth and citation depth diverge. Perplexity and Google cite more sources on average, while ChatGPT cites fewer sources but shows substantially higher average citation influence among fetched pages. High-influence pages tend to be longer, more structured, semantically aligned, and richer in extractable evidence such as definitions, numerical facts, comparisons, and procedural steps. The results suggest that GEO should be measured beyond citation counts, with answer-level absorption treated as a separate outcome.

</details>


### [118] [Personalized Multi-Interest Modeling for Cross-Domain Recommendation to Cold-Start Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25732)
*Xiaodong Li,Jiawei Sheng,Jiangxia Cao,Xinghua Zhang,Wenyuan Zhang,Yong Sun,Shirui Pan,Zhihong Tian,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出NF-NPCDR模型，利用归一化流增强神经过程，通过个性化偏好编码器捕获用户多兴趣偏好，结合公共偏好编码器和随机自适应解码器，解决跨域推荐中冷启动用户的个性化多兴趣建模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐方法大多忽略用户的个性化偏好和多兴趣特性，且未能有效利用用户间的共同偏好信息。

Method: 提出NF-NPCDR框架，包含：（1）个性化偏好编码器，利用归一化流(NF)增强神经过程(NP)，将高斯单峰分布转换为多峰分布以捕获用户多兴趣；（2）公共偏好编码器，通过偏好池捕获用户间共同偏好；（3）随机自适应解码器，自适应融合个性化与公共偏好进行推荐。

Result: 该方法能有效建模用户个性化多兴趣偏好，同时利用用户间共同偏好信息，提升冷启动用户的跨域推荐性能。

Conclusion: NF-NPCDR通过多峰分布建模和偏好融合机制，为跨域冷启动推荐提供了更精准的个性化解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) has demonstrated to be an effective solution for alleviating the user cold-start issue. By leveraging rich user-item interactions available in a richly informative source domain, CDR could improve the recommendation performance for cold-start users in the target domain. Previous CDR approaches mostly adhere the Embedding and Mapping (EMCDR) paradigm, which learns a user-shared mapping function to transfer users' preference from the source domain to the target domain, neglecting users' personalized preference. Recent CDR approaches further leverage the meta-learning paradigm, considering the CDR task for each user independently and learning user-specific mapping functions for each user. However, they mostly learn representations for each user individually, which ignores the common preference between different users, neglecting valuable information for CDR. In addition, all these approaches usually summarize the user's preference into an overall representation, which can hardly capture the user's multi-interest preference. To this end, we propose a personalized multi-interest modeling framework for CDR to cold-start users, termed as NF-NPCDR. Specifically, we propose a personalized preference encoder that enhances the neural process (NP) with the normalizing flow (NF) to convert the Gaussian (unimodal) distribution to a multimodal distribution, providing a novel way to capture the user's personalized multi-interest preference. Then, we propose a common preference encoder with a preference pool to capture the common preference between different users. Furthermore, we introduce a stochastic adaptive decoder to incorporate both the personalized and common preference for cold-start users, adaptively modulating both preference for better recommendation.

</details>


### [119] [Break the Inaccessible Boundary: Distilling Post-Conversion Content for User Retention Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25839)
*Tianbao Ma,Ruochen Yang,Chengen Li,Yuexin Shi,Jiangxia Cao,Linxun Chen,Zhaojie Liu,Yanan Niu,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对RTB广告系统中用户留存预测的onboarding content特征泄露问题，提出两阶段蒸馏对齐框架OCARM，通过教师模型暴露未来内容训练、学生模型通过蒸馏近似特征，在不泄露的情况下提升留存预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RTB广告重定向系统中，onboarding content（用户转化后的内容消费）对留存预测极具信息量，但直接用于训练会导致特征泄露，即训练时使用未来信息而推理时不可用，造成训练与服务之间的gap。

Method: 两阶段蒸馏对齐框架OCARM：第一阶段，有意暴露onboarding content训练层次编码器生成教师表示；第二阶段，通过蒸馏将用户编码器与冻结的教师模型对齐，使模型在推理时仅用可观测特征隐式近似不可观测的onboarding signals，避免特征泄露。

Result: 离线实验和在线A/B测试均表明该框架在真实增长场景中取得一致的改进效果。

Conclusion: OCARM有效解决了onboarding content的特征泄露问题，通过蒸馏对齐实现了在推理时无需未来信息即可捕获对留存预测有价值的信号，提升了留存预测的准确性。

Abstract: User retention is a key metric to measure long-term engagement in modern platforms. In real-time bidding (RTB) advertising system for user re-engagement, the retention model is required to predict future revisit probability at bidding time, before the user converts and consumes any content. Although post-conversion content, termed Onboarding Content, provides highly informative signals for retention prediction, directly using it in training causes severe feature leakage and creates a gap between training and serving. To address this issue, we propose OCARM, a two-stage distillation-aligned framework for Onboarding Content Augmented Retention Modeling, enabling the model to implicitly capture future content using only observable features during inference. In the first stage, we deliberately expose onboarding content to train a hierarchical encoder that produces teacher representations. In the second stage, a user encoder is aligned with the frozen teacher through distillation, allowing the model to approximate the inaccessible onboarding signals without leakage. Extensive offline experiments and online A/B tests demonstrate that our framework achieves consistent improvements in a real-world growth scenario.

</details>


### [120] [Make Any Collection Navigable: Methods for Constructing and Evaluating Hypergraph of Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25906)
*Dean E. Alvarez,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何构建和评估文本超图（Hypergraph of Text, HoT），并提出了一种新的定量指标——努力比（effort ratio），实验表明简单的TF-IDF基线方法在努力比指标上可与基于LLM的方法相媲美。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超链接通常手动创建，无法充分捕捉语料库的隐式语义结构，因此需要一种通用的方法来使任意文档集合具备可导航性。

Method: 提出了多种构建文本超图（HoT）的方法，并提出了一种新的定量评估指标——努力比（effort ratio）来评估所构建HoT的结构质量。

Result: 在提出的努力比指标上，即使简单的TF-IDF基线方法也能与基于LLM的方法表现相当。

Conclusion: 文本超图是支持导航和浏览的形式化数学结构，但其构建和评估仍具挑战性；努力比指标为评估超图结构质量提供了有效手段，且简单方法在该指标上具有竞争力。

Abstract: One reason the Web is more useful than a simple collection of documents is that the structure created by hyperlinks enables flexible navigation from one web page to another. However, hyperlinks are typically created manually and cannot fully capture a corpus' implicit semantic structures. Is there a general way to make an arbitrary collection navigable? Recent work has formalized this problem generally as constructing a Hypergraph of Text (HoT), which provides a formal mathematical structure for supporting navigation and browsing. However, how to construct and evaluate a Hypergraph of Text remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose and study several methods for constructing a HoT. We also propose a novel quantitative metric, effort ratio, for evaluating the structural quality of a constructed HoT. Experimental results show that even simple TF-IDF baselines can match LLM-based methods on our proposed effort ratio metric.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [GCA-BULF: A Bottom-Up Framework for Short-Term Load Forecasting Using Grouped Critical Appliances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24766)
*Yunhao Yao,Jinwei Fang,Puhan Luo,Zhiqiang Wang,Jiahui Hou,Xiang-Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GCA-BULF，一种基于分组关键电器的自下而上短期负荷预测框架，通过关键电器筛选、相关电器分组和协同负荷预测三个模块，在住宅和办公楼负荷预测任务上相比现有自上而下方法提升20.85%-57.88%，相比自下而上方法提升33.03%-92.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分时电价和阶梯电价背景下，传统自上而下预测方法难以捕捉混合电器的复杂负荷模式；自下而上方法虽能提升精度，但监控所有电器成本高昂且许多电器对总负荷预测贡献不大，因此需要一种成本可控、精度更高的短期负荷预测方法。

Method: 提出GCA-BULF框架，包含三个关键设计：(1) 关键电器筛选模块：根据功耗、开关频率和用电模式周期性对电器排序，通过迭代负荷分解识别关键电器；(2) 相关电器分组模块：基于时空相关性对关键电器聚类，进行组级预测；(3) 协同负荷预测模块：融合多组预测结果精化总负荷预测。

Result: 在住宅和办公楼负荷预测任务上，GCA-BULF相比现有自上而下方法提升20.85%-57.88%，相比自下而上方法提升33.03%-92.48%。

Conclusion: GCA-BULF通过识别和分组关键电器进行协同预测，在显著降低监控成本的同时大幅提升了短期负荷预测精度，验证了基于关键电器的自下而上方法的有效性。

Abstract: With the rise of time-of-use and tiered electricity pricing, energy consumers are encouraged to adopt peak-shifting strategies by automatically controlling high-power appliances. These help lower energy costs while enhancing the power grid's stability. To support such energy management with high resilience and responsiveness, reliable short-term load forecasting (STLF) plays a critical role. STLF predicts electricity consumption over time horizons ranging from minutes to days, using historical data, temporal patterns, and contextual factors. Traditional top-down forecasting methods struggle to capture the complex consumption patterns of diverse and mixed appliance loads. Although bottom-up methods improve forecasting accuracy by integrating appliance-level data, monitoring all appliances is costly, and many do not meaningfully impact total load prediction. Therefore, we propose GCA-BULF, a bottom-up short-term load forecasting framework based on grouped critical appliances, supported by three key designs. First, the Critical Appliance Filtering module ranks appliances according to their power consumption, switching frequency, and usage pattern periodicity, and identifies critical ones through iterative load decomposition. Next, the Related Appliance Grouping module clusters these appliances based on spatial and temporal correlations for group-level forecasting. Finally, the Collaborative Load Forecasting module refines the total load prediction by combining multiple group-level forecasts. We evaluate GCA-BULF on residential and office building load forecasting tasks. Experimental results reveal that GCA-BULF improves hourly total load forecasting by 20.85%-57.88% compared to existing top-down methods and by 33.03%-92.48% compared to bottom-up methods.

</details>


### [122] [Automated detection of pediatric congenital heart disease from phonocardiograms using deep and handcrafted feature fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24767)
*Abdul Jabbar,Ethan Grooby,Yang Yi Poh,Khawza I. Ahmad,Md Hassanuzzaman,Raqibul Mostafa,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Faezeh Marzbanrad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度特征融合的数字听诊器方法，利用心音图记录从751名孟加拉国儿科受试者中自动检测先天性心脏病，达到了92%的准确率、91%的敏感性和特异性，以及96%的AUROC，为低资源环境提供了一种经济高效的远程筛查工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先天性心脏病是最常见的出生缺陷，影响全球约1%的活产婴儿。金标准诊断方法超声心动图成本高且在低资源环境中难以获取，加之缺乏足够的专业专家且存在临床医生间判读差异，导致诊断延迟。因此，需要一种更易获取、成本更低的诊断方式。

Method: 提出基于深度特征融合的方法，整合深度特征与手工特征，用于心音图记录的自动早期CHD检测。数据来自孟加拉国751名儿科受试者（年龄1个月-16岁），在四个听诊位置（二尖瓣、主动脉瓣、肺动脉瓣、三尖瓣）采集，并由心脏病专家确诊标注为CHD或非CHD。采用70%训练、20%验证、10%测试的患者级划分。

Result: 模型在测试集上达到92%准确率、91%敏感性、91%特异性、96%AUROC和92%F1分数。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为低资源环境中一种经济有效的远程CHD筛查工具，具有高效的实时检测潜力。

Abstract: Congenital heart disease (CHD) is the most common type of birth defect, impacting about 1% of live births worldwide. Echocardiography, the gold-standard diagnostic method, is costly and inaccessible in low-resource settings. Diagnosis is delayed due to limited skilled experts, whose ability to interpret pathological patterns varies significantly, causing inter- and intra-clinician variability. Therefore, we present a new method for a more accessible diagnostic modality, the digital stethoscope, to detect CHDs. Our method is based on deep feature fusion, integrating deep and handcrafted features for the automated early detection of CHDs. For this work, Phonocardiography (PCG) recordings were obtained from 751 pediatric subjects (Age:1 month- 16 years) in Bangladesh, ranging from infants to adults at four auscultation locations: mitral valve (MV), aortic valve (AV), pulmonary valve (PV), and tricuspid valve (TV). These recordings were labeled based on confirmed diagnoses by cardiologists as either cases of CHD or non-CHD. The results demonstrated that our proposed model achieved an accuracy of 92%, a sensitivity of 91%, and a specificity of 91%, based on a patient-wise split of 70% training, 20% validation, and 10% testing. Furthermore, the Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 96%, and an F1-score of 92%. This model promises efficient real-time remote detection of CHDs as a cost-effective screening tool for low-resource settings.

</details>


### [123] [Architecture Determines Observability in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24801)
*Thomas Carmichael*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义并研究了“可观测性”——从冻结的中间层激活中线性读取每token决策质量的能力，并发现该属性并非transformer的通用特性；架构和训练配方决定了模型内部是否保留决策质量信号，且该信号与输出置信度正交。在多种模型家族中均观测到某些配置（如Pythia的24层16头）会坍塌至低可观测性，而其他配置则保持健康信号。训练过程中信号坍塌是涌现现象，且可观测性差异在不同架构间跨度达2.9倍以上。基于可观测性训练的错误检测器可在下游任务中捕获置信度漏检的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归transformer会犯“自信错误”，即输出高置信度但实际错误的预测。虽然激活监测可能捕捉这些错误，但这要求模型内部保留输出置信度未暴露的决策质量信号。然而该保留性取决于架构和训练配方，且现有研究缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在量化这种内部信号的可读性，并揭示架构选择对监控能力的影响。

Method: 定义“可观测性”为在控制最大softmax置信度和激活范数后，从冻结的中间层激活中线性预测每token决策质量的偏相关系数。在6个模型家族（Pythia、Qwen、Llama、Mistral等）的13个模型上进行系统实验，使用探针（probe）读取中间层状态，并通过偏相关分析剥离置信度和范数的混杂效应。在Pythia控制套件中对比不同深度-头数配置，并跟踪训练检查点的信号演化。最后训练WikiText上的可观测性探针，迁移到下游QA任务测试错误检测能力。

Result: 1) 置信度控制吸收57.7%的原始探针信号，偏相关系数校正至关重要。2) Pythia中24层16头配置在所有测试运行中坍塌至rho_partial~0.10，而其他6种配置处于0.21-0.38的健康区间，坍塌点与输出控制残差坍塌一致，非线性探针和层扫描均无法恢复。3) 坍塌在训练过程中涌现：两种配置在最早检查点均形成信号，但(24L,16H)类在训练中信号被抹除而预测损失持续改善。4) 跨独立配方坍塌图谱变化：Qwen 2.5与Llama在3B规模差异2.9倍且探针种子分布不重叠，Mistral 7B保持可观测性而Llama 3.1 8B坍塌。5) WikiText训练的可观测性探针迁移到下游QA，以20%标记率捕获置信度漏检的10.9-13.4%错误。

Conclusion: 可观测性不是transformer的通用属性，架构选择本质上是一个监控决策。即使采用复杂探针或层扫描，某些架构（如Pythia 24L16H、Llama 3.1 8B）也无法恢复内部决策质量信号，而另一些（如Mistral 7B、Qwen 2.5特定规模）则保持良好可观测性。这为模型部署中的监控可行性提供了架构层面的指导原则。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers make confident errors, but activation monitoring can catch them only if the model preserves an internal signal that output confidence does not expose. This preservation is determined by architecture and training recipe. We define observability as the linear readability of per-token decision quality from frozen mid-layer activations after controlling for max-softmax confidence and activation norm. The correction is essential. Confidence controls absorb 57.7% of raw probe signal on average across 13 models in 6 families.
  Observability is not a generic property of transformers. In Pythia's controlled suite, every tested run with the 24-layer, 16-head configuration collapses to rho_partial ~0.10 across a 3.5x parameter gap and two Pile variants, while six other configurations occupy a separated healthy band from 0.21 to 0.38. The output-controlled residual collapses at the same points, and neither tested nonlinear probes nor layer sweeps recover healthy-range signal. Checkpoint dynamics show the collapse is emergent during training. Both configurations at matched hidden dimension form the signal at the earliest measured checkpoint, but training erases it in the (24L, 16H) class while predictive loss continues improving.
  Across independent recipes the collapse map changes but the phenomenon persists. Qwen 2.5 and Llama differ by 2.9x at matched 3B scale with probe seed distributions that do not overlap, while Mistral 7B preserves observability where Llama 3.1 8B collapses despite similar broad architecture. A WikiText-trained observer transfers to downstream QA without training on those tasks, catching errors confidence misses. At 20% flag rate, its exclusive catch rate is 10.9-13.4% of all errors in seven of nine model-task cells. Architecture selection is a monitoring decision.

</details>


### [124] [Intrinsic Mutual Information as a Modulator for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24804)
*Peng Liao,Peijia Zheng,Lingbo Li,Shangsong Liang,Lin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RMiPO框架，利用响应层面的互信息进行离线偏好优化，在减少超参数调优开销的同时提升模型对齐性能，训练开销降低15%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线偏好优化方法（如DPO）虽能有效对齐大语言模型与人类价值观，但通常需要额外的超参数调优，导致大量时间开销。先前改进方法未能完全消除对超参数调优的依赖。

Method: 提出RMiPO框架，利用响应层面的互信息（Response-level Mutual information）进行偏好优化，通过动态解耦偏好贡献，在几乎不计额外计算成本的情况下实现超参数调制。

Result: RMiPO在多个任务上 consistently 优于现有方法，同时训练开销降低超过15%。

Conclusion: RMiPO是一种轻量高效的离线偏好优化框架，有效消除了超参数调优依赖，提升了模型对齐性能并降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), offer significant advantages in aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with human values. However, achieving optimal performance with these methods typically involves additional hyperparameter tuning, resulting in substantial time overhead. Although prior work has proposed a range of improvements, these methods remain limited in effectiveness and have not fully eliminated reliance on hyperparameter tuning. In this work, we propose RMiPO, a lightweight and efficient framework for offline preference optimization. RMiPO leverages intrinsic Response-level Mutual information for Preference Optimization with hyperparameter modulation, dynamically decoupling preference contributions at negligible additional computational cost. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that RMiPO achieves consistently superior performance over existing methods while reducing training overhead by more than 15\%. Our code is available at https://github.com/liavonpenn/rmipo.

</details>


### [125] [minAction.net: Energy-First Neural Architecture Design -- From Biological Principles to Systematic Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24805)
*Martin G. Frasch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过2203次实验发现：架构本身对精度解释力极弱（partial eta^2=0.001），而架构×数据集交互效应显著（partial eta^2=0.44）；能量正则化损失函数可将内部激活能量降至基线6%且精度无损；基于作用量原理的能量优先架构带来5-33%的训练效率提升。该工作将经典力学作用量、统计物理自由能与变分推断中的KL正则化目标之间的结构对应作为设计假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习优化精度时未考虑内部计算成本，但物理与生物系统均受内在能量约束，故需要研究能量感知学习。

Method: 在视觉、文本、神经形态和生理数据集上进行了2203次实验（每种配置10个种子），采用因子统计分析；提出能量正则化目标L = L_CE + λ * E(θ, x)；基于作用量原理设计能量优先架构。

Result: 架构对精度的解释方差极小（partial eta^2=0.001），架构×数据集交互效应大（partial eta^2=0.44, p<0.001）；能量正则化在适度λ下将能量降至基线6%而无精度损失；能量优先架构带来5-33%的训练效率提升。

Conclusion: 最优架构高度依赖任务模态，不存在普适最优架构；能量正则化可大幅降低计算成本且不牺牲精度；将学习过程解释为经典力学作用量、统计物理自由能与变分推断KL正则化之间的结构对应是一种有价值的设计假设。

Abstract: Modern machine learning optimizes for accuracy without explicitly accounting for internal computational cost, even though physical and biological systems operate under intrinsic energy constraints. We evaluate energy-aware learning across 2,203 experiments spanning vision, text, neuromorphic, and physiological datasets, using 10 seeds per configuration and performing a factorial statistical analysis. Three findings emerge. First, architecture alone explains negligible variance in accuracy (partial eta^2 = 0.001). In contrast, the architecture x dataset interaction is large (partial eta^2 = 0.44, p < 0.001), demonstrating that optimal architecture depends critically on task modality and rejecting the assumption of a universal best architecture. Second, a controlled lambda-sweep over four orders of magnitude validates a single-parameter energy-regularized objective L = L_CE + lambda * E(theta, x): internal activation energy decreases to 6% of baseline at moderate lambda with no accuracy degradation on MNIST. Third, energy-first architectures inspired by an action-principle framework yield 5-33% within-modality training-efficiency gains over conventional baselines. These results emerge from a research program that interprets learning through a structural correspondence between the action functional in classical mechanics, free energy in statistical physics, and KL-regularized objectives in variational inference. We frame this correspondence as a design hypothesis rather than a derivation.

</details>


### [126] [Nautile-370M: Spectral Memory Meets Attention in a Small Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24809)
*Maixent Chenebaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nautile-370M是一个3.71亿参数的小型语言模型，采用混合骨干架构（两个SeqCond Attention层与一个Transformer层交替），在严格参数和推理预算下实现高效推理。模型通过数学证明SCA读出机制能精确检索前缀摘要中的任意token，且在连续极限下可再现softmax注意力的任何输出。训练在Cloud TPU v4-64和NVIDIA DGX Spark上进行，并设计了面向推理、验证和响应质量的强化学习阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的参数规模和推理预算约束下，设计一个既能保持长上下文效率和状态追踪能力（结构化序列模型优势），又能保留注意力机制中token间路由表达能力的小型语言模型。

Method: 采用混合骨干架构：两个SeqCond Attention（SCA）层（受SeqCondenser启发的线性时间谱序列算子）与一个Transformer层交替堆叠。模型参数为3.71亿，训练在TPU v4-64 pod slice上，强化学习阶段在单个NVIDIA DGX Spark上完成。

Result: 数学上证明了SCA读出机制可以精确检索前缀摘要中的任意token，并且在连续极限下能再现softmax注意力的任何输出，表明SCA在表达能力上至少与全自注意力等价。

Conclusion: SCA注意力机制在小型语言模型中具备与传统全自注意力相当的表达能力，同时保持结构化序列模型的高效性，为资源受限场景下的推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present Nautile-370M, a 371-million-parameter small language model designed for efficient reasoning under strict parameter and inference budgets. Nautile-370M uses a hybrid backbone in which two SeqCond Attention (SCA) layers, a linear-time spectral sequence operator inspired by SeqCondenser, alternate with one transformer layer. This design aims to retain the long-context efficiency and state-tracking benefits of structured sequential models while preserving the expressive token-to-token routing of attention. The model was trained on a single Cloud TPU v4-64 pod slice provided through the Google TPU Research Cloud (TRC) program; the subsequent reinforcement learning stage was carried out on a single NVIDIA DGX Spark. We prove that the SCA readout mechanism can exactly retrieve any individual token from the prefix summary and can reproduce any output of softmax attention as a special case, establishing that SCA is at least as expressive as full self-attention in the continuous limit. We also describe the training data pipeline and outline a reinforcement learning stage specialized for reasoning, verification, and response quality.

</details>


### [127] [Time-varying Interaction Graph ODE for Dynamic Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24811)
*Xiaoyi Wang,Zhiqiang Wang,Jianqing Liang,Xingwang Zhao,Chuangyin Dang,Zhen Jin,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TI-ODE通过将图神经ODE的演化函数分解为一组可学习的交互基函数，并使用时变权重动态组合，从而捕获动态图中节点交互模式的多样性和时变性，在六个动态图数据集上取得最优属性预测性能，并展现出更强的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经ODE在动态图场景中采用统一的消息传递机制，假设节点间交互共享相同的消息传递函数，难以捕获交互模式的多样性和时变性。

Method: 将图ODE的演化函数分解为一组可学习的交互基函数，每个基函数对应一种不同的节点间交互类型，并通过时间依赖的可学习权重动态组合这些基函数，使交互模式能随时间自适应演化。

Result: 在六个动态图数据集上TI-ODE持续超越现有方法，在属性预测任务上达到最优性能；在Covid数据集上验证了可解释性和泛化性；理论和实验均证明TI-ODE比使用统一消息传递机制的模型具有更优的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TI-ODE通过动态组合交互基函数有效建模了动态图中随时间演化的节点交互模式，同时提升了预测性能、鲁棒性和可解释性。

Abstract: Graph neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODE) combine neural ODE with the message passing mechanism of Graph Neural Networks (GNN), providing a continuous-time modeling method for graph representation learning. However, in dynamic graph scenarios, existing graph neural ODEs typically employ a unified message passing mechanism, assuming that inter-node interactions share the same message passing function at any time, which makes it challenging to capture the diversity and time-varying nature of inter-node interaction patterns. To address this, we propose Time-varying Interaction Graph Ordinary Differential Equations (TI-ODE). The core idea of TI-ODE is to decompose the evolution function of a graph ODE into a set of learnable interaction basis functions, where each basis function corresponds to a distinct type of inter-node interaction. These basis functions are dynamically combined through time-dependent learnable weights, enabling inter-node interaction patterns to adaptively evolve over time. Experimental results on six dynamic graph datasets demonstrate that TI-ODE consistently outperforms existing methods and achieves state-of-the-art performance on attribute prediction tasks, and experiments on the \textit{Covid} dataset further verify the interpretability and generalizability of our TI-ODE. Furthermore, we demonstrate both theoretically and empirically that TI-ODE exhibits superior robustness compared to models utilizing a unified message-passing mechanism.

</details>


### [128] [Heterogeneous Variational Inference for Markov Degradation Hazard Models: Discretized Mixture with Interpretable Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24818)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对贝叶斯有限混合模型在工业设备风险聚类中的三大瓶颈（粗粒度离散化导致退化信号不足、聚类不稳定、MCMC计算不可行），提出了融合8状态全局百分位离散化、30维特征工程、可解释模型选择规则及ADVI全秩变分推断的实用框架。在280台工业泵设备的104,703条检测记录上验证，ADVI相比NUTS实现84倍加速且更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决贝叶斯有限混合模型在工业设备风险聚类中的三个关键瓶颈：粗状态离散化造成退化信号不足、数据支持更少聚类时识别不稳定、MCMC方法（7+小时/模型）无法用于生产部署。

Method: (1) 8状态全局百分位离散化增强退化事件；(2) 30维特征工程（22个统计趋势特征+连续健康指标+经PCA压缩至3维的文本嵌入）；(3) 可解释模型选择规则（最小聚类份额、聚类分离度+WAIC）；(4) 采用全秩协方差ADVI进行稳定快速估计。

Result: ADVI与NUTS在随机效应模型上估计几乎一致（15倍加速）；有限混合模型中ADVI比NUTS稳定84倍且更快；NUTS存在严重收敛问题和标签切换。

Conclusion: (1) 首次证明细粒度状态离散化（8状态）对生存分析中混合模型稳定性至关重要；(2) 提出统计、连续、语义信号综合特征工程策略；(3) 提出防止过拟合的可解释模型选择规则；(4) 实证ADVI在收敛性、稳定性和计算效率上优于NUTS。

Abstract: Bayesian finite mixture models can identify discrete risk clusters (low-risk vs. high-risk equipment), but face three critical bottlenecks: (1) insufficient degradation signals from coarse state discretization, (2) unstable cluster identification when data inherently supports fewer clusters than explored, and (3) computational infeasibility of Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods for production deployment (7+ hours per model). We propose a practical framework combining (1) 8-state global percentile discretization that amplifies degradation events, (2) 30-dimensional feature engineering integrating statistical trends (22 features), continuous health indicators, and text embeddings (PCA-compressed to 3 dimensions), (3) interpretable model selection rules enforcing minimum cluster share and separation alongside WAIC, and (4) Automatic Differentiation Variational Inference (ADVI) with full-rank covariance for stable, fast estimation. Applied to 280 industrial pump equipment with 104,703 inspection records, we demonstrate: (1) Random effect models (baseline) show ADVI and NUTS produce nearly identical estimates with 15$\times$ speedup, validating ADVI accuracy. (2) Finite mixture models identify optimal number of clusters with interpretability constraints. (3) NUTS exhibits severe convergence issues and label switching, while ADVI provides stable results in 84$\times$ less time. We contributed that (1) First demonstration that fine-grained state discretization (8-state) is essential for mixture model stability in survival analysis.(2) Comprehensive feature engineering strategy combining statistical, continuous, and semantic signals. (3) Practical interpretability rules preventing overfitting in automated model selection. (4) Empirical evidence that ADVI outperforms NUTS for finite mixture models in terms of convergence, stability, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [129] [Negative Ontology of True Target for Machine Learning: Towards Evaluation and Learning under Democratic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24824)
*Yongquan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从哲学角度审视了“真实目标（TT）”存在性假设的转变如何为基于机器学习的预测建模带来新视角，并基于TT不存在（负本体论）的假设，提出了“民主监督”下的评估与学习知识体系，具体通过多重不准确真实目标（MIATTs）实现，构建了EL-MIATTs框架，并在教育领域展示了应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流机器学习范式大多假设真实目标（TT）客观存在，但这一假设在复杂现实任务中（如教育评估）往往不成立。作者旨在通过质疑TT的存在性假设，探索一种不依赖客观TT的新的ML范式，以解决实际建模中的根本性局限。

Method: 采用负本体论立场（认为TT不存在），定义了民主监督；提出MIATTs（多重不准确真实目标）作为实例级实现；基于MIATTs给出了逻辑驱动的生成与评估原则、逻辑评估公式以及不可定义真实目标学习机制；最终构建了EL-MIATTs框架。

Result: 建立了EL-MIATTs框架，通过真实应用案例展示了该框架在支持个体教育和专业发展方面的潜力，与教育及专业发展领域的民主监督讨论相呼应。

Conclusion: 通过否定TT的客观存在性，民主监督及EL-MIATTs框架为ML预测建模提供了一种全新的知识体系，尤其在缺乏客观标准的教育等场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: This article philosophically examines how shifts in assumptions regarding the existence and non-existence of the true target (TT) give rise to new perspectives and insights for machine learning (ML)-based predictive modeling and, correspondingly, proposes a knowledge system for evaluation and learning under Democratic Supervision. By systematically analysing the existence assumption of the TT in current mainstream ML paradigms, we explicitly adopt a negative ontology perspective, positing that the TT does not objectively exist in the real world, and, grounded in this non-existence assumption, define Democratic Supervision for ML. We further present Multiple Inaccurate True Targets (MIATTs) as an instance-level realization of Democratic Supervision. Building upon MIATTs, we derive principles, for the logic-driven generation and assessment of MIATTs, a logical assessment formulation for evaluation with MIATTs, and undefinable true target learning for learning with MIATTs. Based on these components, we establish the evaluation and learning with MIATTs (EL-MIATTs) framework for ML-based predictive modelling. A real-world application demonstrates the potential of the proposed EL-MIATTs framework in supporting education and professional development for individuals, aligning with prior discussions of Democratic Supervision in the fields of education and professional development.

</details>


### [130] [Incompressible Knowledge Probes: Estimating Black-Box LLM Parameter Counts via Factual Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24827)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出不可压缩知识探针（IKP），通过测量模型实际知道的事实数量来下界估计其参数量，发现知识容量随参数呈对数线性增长，且不受推理基准饱和与推理经济不确定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭源前沿模型不公开参数量，而推理经济学的估计存在2倍以上的不确定性（来自硬件、批处理、服务栈等外部假设）。需要一种更紧的内在约束来估计模型参数量。

Method: 设计了包含1,400个事实问题的IKP基准，覆盖7个难度层级，确保问题无法通过推理推导或架构改进压缩。在89个开源模型（135M-1,600B参数）上校准了IKP准确率与参数量的对数线性映射（R²=0.917），并进行留一交叉验证。对MoE模型分别分析总参数与激活参数的预测能力。评估了188个模型（27个厂商），包括所有主要闭源前沿模型。

Result: IKP准确率与参数量呈强对数线性关系（R²=0.917）；留一交叉验证中位数误差1.59倍，68.5%在2倍内，87.6%在3倍内。MoE模型中总参数预测知识（R²=0.79）远优于激活参数（R²=0.51）。时间系数为-0.0010/月（95% CI [-0.0031, +0.0008]），与零无显著差异，显著拒绝了"密集化定律"预测的+0.0117/月（p<10^−15）。知识容量随参数对数线性增长，跨代际、跨厂商一致。

Conclusion: 广泛报道的推理基准饱和并不意味着缩放定律的终结。程序能力可能被压缩（密集化定律），但事实知识容量仍在随参数量对数线性增长。对于安全微调后的模型，IKP估计是下界，因为拒答策略可能隐藏大量"知道但拒绝回答"的知识。

Abstract: Closed-source frontier labs do not disclose parameter counts, and the standard alternative -- inference economics -- carries $2\times$+ uncertainty from hardware, batching, and serving-stack assumptions external to the model. We exploit a tighter intrinsic bound: storing $F$ facts requires at least $F/$(bits per parameter) weights, so measuring how much a model \emph{knows} lower-bounds how many parameters it \emph{has}. We introduce \textbf{Incompressible Knowledge Probes (IKPs)}, a benchmark of 1{,}400 factual questions spanning 7 tiers of obscurity, designed to isolate knowledge that cannot be derived by reasoning or compressed by architectural improvements.
  We calibrate a log-linear mapping from IKP accuracy to parameter count on 89 open-weight models (135M--1,600B) spanning 19 vendors, achieving $R^2 = 0.917$; leave-one-out cross-validation confirms generalization (median fold error $1.59\times$, $68.5\%$ within $2\times$ and $87.6\%$ within $3\times$). For Mixture-of-Experts models, total parameters predict knowledge ($R^2 = 0.79$) far better than active parameters ($R^2 = 0.51$). We evaluate 188 models from 27 vendors and estimate effective knowledge capacity for all major proprietary frontier models; for heavily safety-tuned models the estimates are lower bounds, since refusal policy can hide tens of percentage points of "refused but known" capacity.
  The widely-reported saturation of reasoning benchmarks does not imply the end of scaling. Procedural capability compresses under the "Densing Law," but across 96 dated open-weight models the IKP time coefficient is $-0.0010$/month (95\% CI $[-0.0031, +0.0008]$) -- indistinguishable from zero, and rejecting the Densing prediction of $+0.0117$/month at $p < 10^{-15}$. Factual capacity continues to scale log-linearly with parameters across generations and across vendors.

</details>


### [131] [On the Trainability of Masked Diffusion Language Models via Blockwise Locality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24832)
*Yuxiang Wang,Yu Xiang,Baojian Zhou,Qifang Zhao,Keyue Jiang,Yanghua Xiao,Xiaoxiao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现标准随机掩码的块级扩散语言模型（MDMs）在有序生成任务中稳定性不如自回归模型，并提出两种带有自回归局部偏置的块级模型（Jigsaw和Scatter）来缓解不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准随机掩码的MDMs在优化上不如自回归大语言模型（AR-LLMs）稳定，现有工作缺乏对MDMs在结构化生成任务中不稳定性的系统性研究。

Method: 在三个受控任务（上下文线性回归、图路径搜索、数独求解）上对比块级MDMs与AR-LLMs；提出两种局部感知块级模型——Jigsaw（块内注入从左到右的自回归偏置）和Scatter（保留扩散规划优势的块级迭代精炼）。

Result: 标准随机掩码MDMs在线性回归上不可靠，图路径搜索训练方差大，数独优于AR-LLMs；Jigsaw在线性回归上匹配AR-LLM的稳定性，Scatter在路径搜索上保留扩散的规划优势。

Conclusion: 标准随机掩码的MDMs（包括块级变体）可能不是有序生成任务的最优扩散LM方案，需要超越随机掩码的模型设计。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to standard autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), yet their optimization can be substantially less stable. We study blockwise MDMs and compare them with AR-LLMs on three controlled tasks that stress different aspects of structured generation: in-context linear regression, graph path-finding, and Sudoku solving. We find that standard random-masking MDMs fail to reliably learn linear regression, exhibit high variance training dynamics on graph path-finding, while outperforming AR-LLMs on Sudoku. To mitigate these instabilities, we propose two locality aware blockwise models, namely Jigsaw and Scatter, that inject left-to-right inductive bias by enforcing autoregressive locality within blocks while preserving iterative refinement at the block level. Empirically, Jigsaw matches AR-LLM stability on linear regression and remains strong on Sudoku, while Scatter retains diffusion's planning advantage on path-finding. Our results indicate that standard random-masking MDMs, even with blockwise variants, may be a suboptimal instantiation of diffusion LMs for ordered generation, motivating models beyond random masking.

</details>


### [132] [Transformer Approximations from ReLUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24878)
*Jerry Yao-Chieh Hu,Mingcheng Lu,Yi-Chen Lee,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种系统化的方法，将ReLU逼近结果迁移到softmax注意力机制中，针对乘法、倒数计算和最小/最大值等常见目标，实现了目标特定且经济高效的资源界限，为分析softmax Transformer模型提供了新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逼近结果多集中于ReLU激活函数，而softmax注意力机制作为Transformer的核心组件，其逼近能力缺乏系统性的分析框架。作者希望建立从ReLU到softmax的翻译方法，以弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出一种系统化配方（recipe），将已有的ReLU逼近结果转化为softmax注意力机制下的逼近结果，并针对不同目标推导出具体的资源界限（如层数、头数等）。

Result: 成功展示了该方法在乘法、倒数计算、最小/最大值等基础运算上的应用，得到了比通用逼近陈述更经济的资源界限。

Conclusion: 该配方为分析softmax Transformer的表示能力提供了新的理论工具，有助于理解Transformer在复杂任务中的计算机制。

Abstract: We provide a systematic recipe for translating ReLU approximation results to softmax attention mechanism. This recipe covers many common approximation targets. Importantly, it yields target-specific, economic resource bounds beyond universal approximation statements. We showcase the recipe on multiplication, reciprocal computation, and min/max primitives. These results provide new analytical tools for analyzing softmax transformer models.

</details>


### [133] [CiteRadar: A Citation Intelligence Platform for Researcher Profiling and Geographic Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25057)
*Chenxu Niu,Yiming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CiteRadar 是一个开源系统，仅需一个 Google Scholar 用户标识符，即可通过命令行自动生成包含完整出版物列表、引用论文及作者元数据、排名表、统计摘要和交互式世界地图的结构化输出文件夹。系统集成了五个数据源，并通过四方面技术贡献解决了引用分析中的关键数据获取与处理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献计量平台要么需要昂贵的机构订阅，要么只提供聚合引用计数而缺乏细粒度的每作者元数据，使得学者难以便捷地了解自身引用的地理分布和社区结构，而这对于职业发展、基金申请和合作发现日益重要。

Method: CiteRadar 采用五阶段流水线架构，集成 Google Scholar、OpenAlex、CrossRef、Semantic Scholar 和 OpenStreetMap Nominatim 五个异构数据源。关键技术包括：(1) 抵御 Unicode 非断行空格分隔符的 Scholar 元字符串解析器；(2) 基于停用词过滤的机构名称相似度两阶段作者消歧系统；(3) OpenAlex 网页URL到API-URL转换修复；(4) 对数刻度交互式 Folium 世界地图生成。

Result: 两阶段作者消歧系统消除了高达9倍正确值的h-index归属错误；OpenAlex URL转换修复将具有城市级位置数据的作者记录比例从0%提升至约60%；系统输出包含完整的出版物列表、引用论文、排名表、统计摘要和自包含的交互式HTML世界地图。

Conclusion: CiteRadar 通过整合多源数据和针对性技术修复，为学者提供了一个易于使用、低成本的引用地理分布可视化和作者社区结构分析工具，有效解决了现有平台在数据粒度和可访问性方面的不足。

Abstract: Understanding the geographic reach and community structure of one's scholarly citations is increasingly valuable for career development, grant applications, and collaboration discovery -- yet accessible tools for answering these questions remain scarce. Existing bibliometric platforms either require costly institutional subscriptions or expose only aggregate citation counts without granular per-author metadata. We present CiteRadar, an open-source system that accepts a single Google Scholar user identifier and automatically produces a structured output folder containing: the author's complete publication list, all retrieved citing papers with enriched author metadata, two ranked author tables (by citation frequency and by h-index), a plain-text statistical summary, and a self-contained interactive HTML world map -- all from a single command-line invocation. CiteRadar integrates five heterogeneous data sources -- Google Scholar, OpenAlex, CrossRef, Semantic Scholar, and OpenStreetMap Nominatim -- through a carefully engineered five-stage pipeline. Key technical contributions include: (1) a Scholar meta-string parser resilient to Unicode non-breaking-space separators, a pervasive but undocumented quirk in Scholar's HTML that silently corrupts venue and year fields when unhandled; (2) a two-stage author disambiguation system using stop-word-filtered institution name similarity to guard against the well-known same-name entity-merging failure mode in bibliometric databases, demonstrated to eliminate h-index attribution errors of up to 9x the correct value; (3) an OpenAlex web-URL to API-URL conversion fix that raises the fraction of author records with city-level location data from 0% to ~60%; and (4) a logarithmically-scaled interactive Folium world map with per-city researcher popups, rendered as a fully self-contained HTML file.

</details>


### [134] [Generative diffusion models for spatiotemporal influenza forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24913)
*Joseph Lemaitre,Justin Lessler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfluPaint 将去噪扩散概率模型应用于传染病预测，将流感季节编码为时空图像，通过条件生成（图像修补）任务实现预报，在回顾性和实时评估中均展现出与领先集成方法相当的准确度，最佳性能由30%监测数据与70%模拟轨迹的混合数据集实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病预测对公共卫生规划至关重要，但现有机械模型和统计方法难以捕捉多模态不确定性及新兴趋势，亟需一种能够灵活建模复杂流行病动态概率分布的预测框架。

Method: 采用去噪扩散概率模型，将流感季节数据编码为时空图像（像素强度代表发病率），利用监测与模拟轨迹的混合数据集训练模型，将预测任务转化为基于部分观测的条件生成（图像修补）问题。

Result: 模型生成真实且多样化的流行轨迹，回顾性评估中预测准确度与领先集成方法相当；2023-2025年美国CDC FluSight挑战赛中性能逐季提升，2024-2025年预测高度准确但略有过自信；30%监测+70%模拟数据组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够捕捉流感动态中重要的时空结构，为概率性传染病预测提供了一个灵活有效的框架。

Abstract: Forecasting infectious disease incidence can provide important information to guide public health planning, yet is difficult because epidemic dynamics are complex. Current mechanistic and statistical approaches often struggle to capture multimodal uncertainty or emergent trends. Influpaint adapts denoising diffusion probabilistic models to epidemic forecasting. By encoding influenza seasons as spatiotemporal images in which pixel intensity represents incidence, Influpaint learns a rich distribution of disease dynamics from a hybrid dataset of surveillance and simulated trajectories. Forecasting is formulated as a conditional generation (inpainting) task from partial observations. We show that Influpaint generates realistic, diverse epidemic trajectories and achieves forecast accuracy that is competitive with leading ensemble methods in retrospective evaluation. In real-time evaluation during the 2023--2025 U.S. CDC FluSight challenges, performance improved substantially across seasons, with highly accurate but somewhat overconfident projections in 2024--2025. The best performance was achieved with a training dataset containing 30% surveillance and 70% simulated trajectories. These results show that diffusion models can capture important spatiotemporal structure in influenza dynamics and provide a flexible framework for probabilistic infectious disease forecasting.

</details>


### [135] [A Unifying Framework for Unsupervised Concept Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24936)
*Chandler Squires,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的无监督概念提取理论框架，将概念提取任务建模为识别生成模型，并通过一个通用元定理简化了可识别性保证的证明过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前概念提取方法（如稀疏自编码器和转码器）在下游任务（如模型引导和遗忘）中被广泛使用，但其保证（或缺乏保证）尚未被充分理解，因此需要建立统一的理论框架来明确这些方法的可识别性保证。

Method: 将概念提取任务形式化为识别生成模型，提出了一个通用的可识别性元定理，将建立可识别性保证的问题简化为刻画两个集合交集的问题，并在多种广泛使用的方法上进行了验证。

Result: 该元定理显著简化了在多种主流概念提取方法上证明可识别性保证的过程，为开发新的、有原则的概念提取方法铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为无监督概念提取提供了理论基础，通过元定理降低了理论分析的门槛，有助于推动更可靠、更有原则的概念提取方法的发展。

Abstract: Techniques for concept extraction, such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders, aim to extract high-level symbolic concepts from low-level nonsymbolic representations. When these extracted concepts are used for downstream tasks such as model steering and unlearning, it is essential to understand their guarantees, or lack thereof. In this work, we present a unified theoretical framework for unsupervised concept extraction, in which we frame the task of concept extraction as identifying a generative model. We present a general meta-theorem for identifiability, which reduces the problem of establishing identifiability guarantees to the problem of characterizing the intersection of two sets. As we demonstrate on a range of widely-used approaches, this meta-theorem substantially simplifies the task of proving such guarantees, thus paving the way for the development of new, principled approaches for concept extraction.

</details>


### [136] [Compute Aligned Training: Optimizing for Test Time Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24957)
*Adam Ousherovitch,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出计算对齐训练（Compute Aligned Training），通过将推理策略视为基策略上的算子，推导出新的损失函数来对齐训练目标与测试时策略，实验证明该方法显著优于标准训练在测试时扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的后训练范式（SFT和RL）优化单个样本在基策略下的似然，这与依赖聚合或过滤输出的测试时程序存在错配，导致测试时计算扩展性能受限。

Method: 提出计算对齐训练方法，将推理策略概念化为基策略上的算子，针对SFT和RL分别推导与常见测试时策略对齐的新损失函数。

Result: 实验证据表明，该训练方法相比标准训练显著提升了测试时计算扩展的性能。

Conclusion: 计算对齐训练能够有效弥合训练目标与测试时策略之间的不一致，从而更充分地利用测试时计算资源提升模型性能。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has emerged as a powerful mechanism for enhancing Large Language Model (LLM) performance. However, standard post-training paradigms, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL), optimize the likelihood of individual samples under a base policy, creating a misalignment with test time procedures that rely on aggregated or filtered outputs. In this work, we propose Compute Aligned Training, which aligns training objectives with test-time strategies. By conceptualizing inference strategies as operators on the base policy, we derive new loss functions that maximize performance when said strategies are applied. We instantiate such loss functions for SFT and RL across common test time strategies. Finally, we provide empirical evidence that this training method substantially improves test time scaling over standard training.

</details>


### [137] [Odysseys: Benchmarking Web Agents on Realistic Long Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24964)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Daniel Fried,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Odysseys基准测试，包含200个基于真实浏览会话的长时域、多站点网页任务。研究发现二元通过/失败评估不适用于长时域场景，引入了基于评分标准的评估方法（平均每个任务6.1个分级评分）。最强模型成功率为44.5%，轨迹效率仅1.15%，表明当前模型在长时域网页任务上仍有巨大改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体基准测试大多集中在短时、单站点的任务上，前沿模型已接近饱和。然而现实世界的网页使用涉及长时域、多站点的工作流，现有基准无法评估智能体在长时间浏览中的跨站点推理和持续上下文保持能力。

Method: 从真实世界浏览会话中提取200个长时域网页任务，在实时互联网上评估。采用基于评分标准的评估方法（平均每个任务6.1个分级评分），替代二元通过/失败评估，并引入轨迹效率指标（评分/步数）。

Result: 最强模型成功率为44.5%；轨迹效率仅1.15%；基于评分标准的评估方法相较于轨迹级LLM-as-a-judge评估与人类一致性更高，提供更细粒度的信号。

Conclusion: 长时域开放网页环境下的智能体能力仍有巨大提升空间，效率应作为首要关注指标。Odysseys为衡量能够在数小时内高效运作的计算机使用智能体的进展提供了现实基准。

Abstract: Existing web agent benchmarks have largely converged on short, single-site tasks that frontier models are approaching saturation on. However, real world web use consists of long-horizon, multi-site workflows. Common web navigation tasks, such as comparing products across different domains, planning trips across multiple services, or summarizing information from multiple search queries, require sustained context and cross-site reasoning over potentially hours of browsing. To capture and evaluate such behaviors, we introduce Odysseys: a benchmark of 200 long-horizon web tasks derived from real world browsing sessions evaluated on the live Internet. We find that binary pass/fail evaluation is inadequate for long-horizon settings and introduce a rubric-based evaluation, annotating each Odysseys task with an average of 6.1 graded rubrics. We demonstrate that this yields higher agreement with humans and provides a more fine-grained signal than commonly used trajectory-level LLM-as-a-judge evaluation metrics. We tested several leading frontier models and find that the strongest models achieve a success rate of 44.5%, which leaves substantial room for future improvements. Beyond task success, we argue that efficiency is a first-class concern for long-horizon agents. We introduce a Trajectory Efficiency metric (rubric score per step) and find that even frontier agents achieve only 1.15%, marking an evident need for agents that can succeed efficiently and not simply eventually. Odysseys isolates the critical evaluation of long-horizon proficiency in open-web environments, providing a realistic benchmark to measure progress towards computer-use agents that can potentially productively operate for hours. We release our tasks, evaluation scripts, and other results at https://odysseys-website.pages.dev

</details>


### [138] [PolyKV: A Shared Asymmetrically-Compressed KV Cache Pool for Multi-Agent LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24971)
*Ishan Patel,Ishan Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyKV 提出多个并发推理智能体共享单个非对称压缩 KV 缓存池的架构，替代传统每智能体独立缓存方案。采用 Keys 的 int8 量化与 Values 的 TurboQuant MSE（FWHT 旋转+3-bit Lloyd-Max 量化）实现非对称压缩。在 Llama-3-8B 上，15 智能体共享 4K 上下文时，KV 缓存从 19.8 GB 降至 0.45 GB（压缩比 2.91x，减少 97.7%），困惑度仅增加 +0.57%，BERTScore F1 达 0.928，且 PPL 增量不随智能体数量增长，长上下文下甚至出现负退化（-0.26%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型推理系统中，每个并发智能体独立维护 KV 缓存，导致随智能体数量线性增长的内存开销巨大，限制了多智能体场景下的可扩展性和资源效率。

Method: 构建单个非对称压缩的 KV 缓存池，通过 HuggingFace DynamicCache 注入 N 个独立智能体上下文。Keys 使用 int8 (q8_0) 量化以保持 softmax 稳定性；Values 使用 TurboQuant MSE 压缩：先经 FWHT 旋转，再进行 3-bit Lloyd-Max 量化，质心优化为 N(0,1) 分布。

Result: 在 SmolLM2-1.7B 和 Llama-3-8B 上，覆盖 600-7,194 token 三种上下文长度及最多 15 智能体，实现稳定 2.91x 压缩比。Llama-3-8B 15 智能体 4K 上下文下，内存从 19.8 GB 降至 0.45 GB（-97.7%），PPL 仅增 +0.57%，BERTScore F1 0.928。PPL 增量不随智能体数增长，长上下文下反转为 -0.26%。

Conclusion: PolyKV 首次将单共享有损压缩 KV 池与多读并发智能体访问相结合，在显著降低内存开销的同时保持生成质量，且随智能体数量和上下文长度增加表现出鲁棒性与性能提升，为多智能体推理基础设施提供了高效的内存共享范式。

Abstract: We present PolyKV, a system in which multiple concurrent inference agents share a single, asymmetrically compressed KV cache pool. Rather than allocating a separate KV cache per agent -- the standard paradigm -- PolyKV writes a compressed cache once and injects it into N independent agent contexts via HuggingFace DynamicCache objects. Compression is asymmetric: Keys are quantized at int8 (q8_0) to preserve softmax stability, while Values are compressed using TurboQuant MSE -- a Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform (FWHT) rotation followed by 3-bit Lloyd-Max quantization with centroids tuned to N(0,1). We evaluate across two model scales (SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct and Llama-3-8B-Instruct), three context lengths (600-7,194 tokens), and up to 15 concurrent agents. PolyKV achieves a stable 2.91x compression ratio across all configurations. On Llama-3-8B with 15 agents sharing a 4K-token context, PolyKV reduces KV cache memory from 19.8 GB to 0.45 GB -- a 97.7% reduction -- while maintaining only +0.57% perplexity degradation and a mean BERTScore F1 of 0.928. PPL delta does not grow with agent count and improves as context length increases, inverting to -0.26% at 1,851 coherent tokens. To our knowledge, no prior work combines a single shared, lossy-compressed KV pool with multi-reader concurrent agent access.

</details>


### [139] [Laplace-Bridged Randomized Smoothing for Fast Certified Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24993)
*Miao Lin,MD Saifur Rahman Mazumder,Feng Yu,Daniel Takabi,Rui Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Laplace-Bridged Smoothing (LBS)，通过将高维输入空间的蒙特卡洛采样替换为低维概率空间的解析计算，在无需噪声增强训练的情况下大幅降低随机平滑的认证成本，在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上取得更强的鲁棒性认证，并在边缘设备上实现高达494倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑在实际部署中存在两大瓶颈：(i) 依赖噪声增强训练才能获得非平凡认证，增加了训练成本、降低了干净精度，削弱了其作为后验防御方法的特性；(ii) 认证过程计算开销巨大，每张输入需要数万次带噪前向传播，在资源受限的边缘设备上难以部署。

Method: 提出Laplace-Bridged Smoothing (LBS)，将随机平滑重新表述为解析形式，用低维概率空间中的高效计算替代高维输入空间中的蒙特卡洛采样，从而无需噪声增强训练即可保留形式化鲁棒性保障，同时大幅降低认证负担。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和ImageNet上，LBS比标准随机平滑获得更强的认证鲁棒性，并将每条样本的认证成本降低近一个数量级。在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano和Raspberry Pi 4上，LBS实现了高达494倍的加速。

Conclusion: LBS通过解析化重新表述有效解决了随机平滑的两大实际瓶颈，为在真实边缘设备上部署认证防御提供了可行方案，并从理论上证明了其解析公式和认证有效性。

Abstract: Randomized Smoothing (RS) offers formal $\ell_2$ guarantees for arbitrary base classifiers but faces two key practical bottlenecks: (i) it often relies on noise-augmented training to achieve nontrivial certificates, which increases training cost, can reduce clean accuracy, and weakens RS as a genuinely post-hoc defense; and (ii) certification is computationally expensive, typically requiring tens of thousands of noisy forward passes per input, which hinders deployment, especially on resource-constrained edge devices. To address both limitations, we propose Laplace-Bridged Smoothing (LBS), an analytic reformulation of RS that replaces high-dimensional input-space Monte Carlo (MC) sampling with efficient computations in a low-dimensional probability space. LBS preserves formal robustness guarantees without requiring noise-augmented training while substantially reducing certification burden. On CIFAR-10 and ImageNet, LBS attains stronger certified robustness than RS and reduces per-sample certification cost by nearly an order of magnitude. Notably, on NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano and Raspberry Pi 4, LBS achieves speedups of up to $494\times$, enabling practical certified deployment on real-world edge devices. Finally, we provide theoretical justification for the analytic formulation and certificate validity of LBS.

</details>


### [140] [Why Search When You Can Transfer? Amortized Agentic Workflow Design from Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25012)
*Shiyi Du,Jiayuan Liu,Weihua Du,Yue Huang,Jiayi Li,Yingtao Luo,Xiangliang Zhang,Vincent Conitzer,Carl Kingsford*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT框架，通过跨任务结构先验知识蒸馏，将智能体工作流设计从迭代搜索转变为单次生成，在保持最优性能的同时将边际优化成本降低三个数量级，且能跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动工作流设计依赖每任务的迭代搜索，计算开销大且无法跨任务复用结构知识。观察到优化后的工作流收敛到少量领域特定拓扑结构，说明组合搜索存在大量冗余。

Method: SWIFT框架包含两个阶段：(1) 从源任务的搜索轨迹中通过对比分析蒸馏组合启发式和输出接口契约；(2) 推理时，将结构先验与跨任务工作流示例一起作为条件输入单个LLM，一次生成完整可执行工作流，完全消除迭代搜索。

Result: 在五个基准上超越最先进的搜索方法，边际优化成本降低三个数量级；泛化到四个未见基准；从GPT-4o-mini成功迁移到Grok、Qwen、Gemma；将算子名称替换为随机字符串仍保留93%以上性能。

Conclusion: 工作流示例主要迁移的是拓扑结构而非表层语义，验证了结构先验的可迁移性和SWIFT框架的有效性。

Abstract: Automated agentic workflow design currently relies on per-task iterative search, which is computationally prohibitive and fails to reuse structural knowledge across tasks. We observe that optimized workflows converge to a small family of domain-specific topologies, suggesting that this combinatorial search is largely redundant. Building on this insight, we propose SWIFT (Synthesizing Workflows via Few-shot Transfer), a framework that amortizes workflow design into reusable structural priors. SWIFT first distills compositional heuristics and output-interface contracts from contrastive analysis of prior search trajectories across source tasks. At inference time, it conditions a single LLM generation pass on these priors together with cross-task workflow demonstrations to synthesize a complete, executable workflow for an unseen target task, bypassing iterative search entirely. On five benchmarks, SWIFT outperforms the state-of-the-art search-based method while reducing marginal per-task optimization cost by three orders of magnitude. It further generalizes to four additional unseen benchmarks and transfers successfully from GPT-4o-mini to three additional foundation models (Grok, Qwen, Gemma). Controlled ablations reveal that workflow demonstrations primarily transfer topological structure rather than surface semantics: replacing all operator names with random strings still retains over 93% of the full system's average performance.

</details>


### [141] [Dynamic Regret for Online Regression in RKHS via Discounted VAW and Subspace Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25021)
*Dmitry B. Rokhlin,Georgiy A. Karapetyants*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了再生核希尔伯特空间中在线回归问题的动态遗憾最小化，通过有限维子空间近似将折扣Vovk-Azoury-Warmuth方法扩展到RKHS设置，并提出了正交截断方法用于不同近似方案，在Gaussian、解析点积核及Matérn核上取得了快慢两种速率下的动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限维折扣VAW方法扩展到RKHS设置，以处理高维或无限维核空间中的在线回归问题，并针对不同核类型建立动态遗憾界。

Method: 1) 通过有限维子空间近似将Jacobsen & Cutkosky (2024)的折扣VAW方法迁移到RKHS；2) 在固定子空间上运行基于几何网格折扣因子的VAW集成；3) 引入正交截断方法：从核的特征展开出发，构造正交基函数并利用前若干个基函数张成的子空间作为近似空间；4) 将该子空间约简应用于不同近似方案（显式特征展开、Mercer截断、核截面张成的子空间）。

Result: Gaussian核和解析点积核获得快速率动态遗憾界；Mercer截断根据特征值衰减速度得到快慢两种速率下的动态遗憾界；Matérn核通过核截面张成的子空间实现有效近似。

Conclusion: 提出了一套系统性的RKHS动态遗憾最小化框架，通过正交截断方法将不同核的特征结构转化为可计算的有限维近似，并证明了在不同核类型下的优势。

Abstract: We study online regression with the square loss in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space under a dynamic regret criterion. The learner is compared with a time-varying comparator sequence, and the bounds depend on its path length in the RKHS norm. The proposed method transfers the finite-dimensional discounted Vovk--Azoury--Warmuth approach of Jacobsen \& Cutkosky (2024) to the RKHS setting by means of finite-dimensional subspace approximations. For a fixed subspace, we run a VAW-based ensemble of discounted VAW forecasters over a geometric grid of discount factors. The additional approximation error is controlled by the uniform projection error of kernel sections.
  We then introduce a general orthogonal truncation method: starting from a feature expansion of the kernel, we construct the associated RKHS by introducing an inner product that makes the feature functions orthonormal, and then use the spans of the first basis functions as finite-dimensional approximation spaces. The resulting subspace reduction is applied to several approximation schemes. Explicit feature expansions yield fast-regime bounds for Gaussian and analytic dot-product kernels. Mercer truncations provide a spectral approximation method and lead to dynamic regret bounds in fast and slow regimes, depending on the eigenvalue decay. Finally, we study subspaces spanned by kernel sections and apply this construction to Matérn kernels.

</details>


### [142] [Null Measurability at the Symmetrization Interface in VC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25028)
*Dhruv Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出标准对称化证明中使用的单侧鬼差上确界只需解析可测性而非Borel可测性，构造了严格分离的例子，证明了弱正则性在概念类构造下的稳定性，并在实可设定下弱化了PAC可学习性的可测性假设。所有结果已在Lean 4中形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视统计学习基本定理中的可测性问题，指出标准对称化证明中实际上只需要比Borel可测性更弱的条件，从而弱化PAC可学习性所需的可测性假设。

Method: 利用解析集理论和Choquet容度定理分析单侧鬼差界面上的可测性；构造反例展示严格分离；证明封闭性定理；在Lean 4中形式化主要结果和描述集合论基础设施。

Result: 证明了对于Polish域上的Borel参数化概念类，坏事件是解析集，因此在每个有限Borel测度的完备化下可测；构造了一个坏事件为可测但非Borel的概念类；证明了弱正则性在修补、固定和可数插值、纤维积合并下封闭。

Conclusion: 弱化了从有限VC维到PAC可学习性的对称化路径所需的可测性假设，为统计学习理论提供了更精细的描述集合论基础。

Abstract: Recent work revisiting measurability in the fundamental theorem of statistical learning imposes Borel measurability of ghost-gap suprema. We show that, at the one-sided ghost-gap interface actually used by the standard symmetrization proof, this requirement is stronger than necessary. For any Borel-parameterized concept class on a Polish domain, the bad event "there exists a hypothesis whose ghost empirical error exceeds its training empirical error by at least ε/2" is analytic. By Choquet capacitability, it is therefore measurable in the completion of every finite Borel measure. We then construct a concept class whose bad event is null-measurable but not Borel, giving a strict separation from the Borel supremum condition. Finally, we prove closure under patching, fixed and countable interpolation, and fiber-product amalgamation, showing that the weaker regularity level is stable under natural concept-class constructors. In the realizable setting, where targets belong to the class and are measurable, these results weaken the measurability hypothesis needed by the symmetrization route from finite VC dimension to PAC learnability. The main results and the descriptive-set-theoretic infrastructure used by them are formalized in Lean 4.

</details>


### [143] [Feasible-First Exploration for Constrained ML Deployment Optimization in Crash-Prone Hierarchical Search Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25073)
*Christian Lysenstøen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA)方法，通过可行性优先的探索阶段与黑名单机制，解决机器学习模型部署优化中因大量无效配置导致的预算浪费问题，在多种GPU上相比冷启动TPE显著提升模型族发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境下的机器学习模型部署需要联合优化模型族、量化方案、运行时后端和服务配置，该搜索空间具有层次混合变量结构且大量配置无效（崩溃、超内存、违反延迟约束）。标准黑盒优化器（如TPE、约束贝叶斯优化）在有效配置常见时表现良好，但在恶劣部署空间中会浪费大量预算在无效或信息量低的试验上。本文旨在研究该场景下是否应将优化分解为显式探索阶段与模型引导的利用阶段。

Method: 提出Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA)，一种可行性优先的探索流程：首先映射有效可行区域，再热启动TPE。包含两种鲁棒性机制：1) 试验超时（提前终止明显不可行的评估）；2) 子空间黑名单（在重复失败后暂时抑制分类子空间）。同时发布DeployBench基准套件，包含层次结构、隐藏崩溃区、硬约束和不均评估成本。

Result: 在合成基准和真实GPU部署（5个预训练视觉模型×5种GPU：H100、A100、RTX 5080、L4、T4）上，所提混合方法在严格预算下提升了模型族发现能力，同时相比冷启动TPE减少了浪费的预算。

Conclusion: 将优化分解为可行性优先的探索阶段再热启动贝叶斯优化，能有效应对恶劣部署空间中的大量无效配置问题，减少预算浪费。

Abstract: Deploying machine learning models under production constraints requires joint optimization over model family, quantization scheme, runtime backend, and serving configuration. This induces a hierarchical mixed-variable search space in which many configurations are invalid: evaluations may crash, exceed memory limits, or violate latency constraints. Standard black-box optimizers such as Tree-structured Parzen Estimators (TPE) and constrained Bayesian optimization are effective when valid configurations are common, but they can spend a large fraction of a small evaluation budget on invalid or uninformative trials in hostile deployment spaces. This paper studies that regime and asks whether optimization should be decomposed into an explicit exploration stage followed by model-guided exploitation. We propose Thermal Budget Annealing (TBA), a feasible-first exploration procedure that maps valid and feasible regions before warm-starting TPE. The method includes two robustness mechanisms for hostile hardware: trial timeouts that abort clearly infeasible evaluations early, and subspace blacklisting that temporarily suppresses categorical subspaces after repeated failures. We also introduce DeployBench, a benchmark suite for deployment optimization with hierarchical structure, hidden crash zones, hard constraints, and unequal evaluation costs. On synthetic benchmarks and real GPU deployment with five pre-trained vision models across five GPU targets (NVIDIA H100, A100, RTX 5080, L4, and T4), the proposed hybrid improves model-family discovery under tight constraints while reducing wasted budget relative to cold-start TPE.

</details>


### [144] [Zero Shot Coordination for Sparse Reward Tasks with Diverse Reward Shapings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25076)
*Keenan Powell,Peihong Yu,Pratap Tokekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体强化学习中零样本协调（ZSC）问题，提出了一种通过随机化奖励塑形和四种选择算法训练集成方法的新方案，在Overcooked环境中与具有相同稀疏奖励但不同奖励塑形的智能体协作时，稀疏奖励提升62.2%-119.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSC解决方案仅假设训练智能体与所有未来伙伴具有完全相同的奖励函数，忽略了实际场景中伙伴虽共享相同稀疏目标却以不同方式塑造奖励的问题，导致训练出的智能体无法适应这种差异。

Method: 使用四种选择算法（具体算法原文未详述）生成随机化奖励塑形，基于这些多样化的奖励塑形训练一个集成方法，使智能体学会与采用不同奖励塑形的伙伴进行零样本协调。

Result: 在Overcooked环境中的实验表明，与使用相同稀疏奖励但不同奖励塑形的智能体协作时，该方法相比基线ZSC算法在稀疏奖励上获得62.2%-119.2%的一致性提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入随机化奖励塑形和集成训练策略，可以有效提升MARL智能体在面对不同奖励塑形伙伴时的零样本协调能力，填补了现有ZSC研究在奖励异质性方面的空白。

Abstract: Many Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) agents fail to adapt properly to cooperating with agents trained with the same objectives but different seeds, algorithms, or other training differences. This is the problem of Zero-Shot Coordination (ZSC), which focuses on training agents to cooperate well with unknown agents. ZSC has been studied for a variety of tabular cases and simple games such as Hanabi, achieving excellent results. However, existing solutions to ZSC only consider identical rewards for your trained agents and all future partners. This is not realistic for the trained agents, as they do not consider the problem of cooperating with agents that have identical sparse objectives but shape the rewards for those objectives in different manner. To address this issue, we show how to train an ensemble of methods using randomized reward shapings chosen using 4 selection algorithms. Experiments done on the Overcooked environment demonstrate consistent improvements of 62.2%-119.2% in sparse reward over baseline ZSC algorithms when playing with agents that have identical sparse rewards but different reward shapings.

</details>


### [145] [Knowledge Distillation Must Account for What It Loses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25110)
*Wenshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出知识蒸馏评估存在“保留假设”缺陷，即仅用任务分数衡量学生模型会掩盖不确定性、边界行为、安全性等关键能力的损失。作者将蒸馏重新定义为有损投影，并系统分类了八类脱指标损失，最后提出场景化保留目标和蒸馏损失报告表，倡导“负责任的蒸馏”而非无损蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏越来越多地用于将大型前沿模型转化为可部署系统，但当前评估仅关注任务分数这类标题指标，忽视了蒸馏过程中模型在不确定性、边界行为、过程可靠性、同策略稳定性、基础能力、隐私、安全性和多样性等方面可能产生的重要损失。作者认为必须对损失的究竟是什么进行问责。

Method: 首先识别当前评估范式背后的“保留假设”（the retention assumption），然后将蒸馏重新概念化为教师行为的“有损投影”（lossy projection）而非忠实复制。接着综合现有证据，构建了八类脱指标蒸馏损失的分类体系（uncertainty, boundary behavior, process reliability, on-policy stability, grounding, privacy, safety, diversity）。最后提出场景化保留目标和“蒸馏损失报告表”（Distillation Loss Statement）作为实操工具。

Result: 系统化归纳了八类难以被传统任务分数捕捉的蒸馏损失；提出了一个可操作的报告框架，要求明确说明蒸馏保留了什么、损失了什么、以及剩余损失为何可接受。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏的目标不应是无损复制（lossless distillation），而是负责任的蒸馏（accountable distillation）——通过明确告知损失内容来保证蒸馏过程的透明性和可信度。

Abstract: This position paper argues that knowledge distillation must account for what it loses: student models should be judged not only by retained task scores, but by whether they preserve the teacher capabilities that make those scores reliable. This matters because distillation is increasingly used to turn large, often frontier models into deployable systems, yet headline metrics can hide losses in uncertainty, boundary behavior, process reliability, on-policy stability, grounding, privacy, safety, and diversity. We identify the retention assumption behind current evaluation and reframe distillation as a lossy projection of teacher behavior rather than a faithful copy. We then synthesize existing evidence into a taxonomy of off-metric distillation losses, showing that these losses are concrete, recurring, and measurable. To make the position actionable, we propose scenario-specific preservation targets and a Distillation Loss Statement that reports what was preserved, what was lost, and why the remaining losses are acceptable. The goal is not lossless distillation, but accountable distillation.

</details>


### [146] [Evaluation without Generation: Non-Generative Assessment of Harmful Model Specialization with Applications to CSAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25119)
*Vinith M. Suriyakumar,Ayush Sekhari,Lena Stempfle,Robertson Wang,Michael Simpson,Rebecca Portnoff,Marzyeh Ghassemi,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯探针（Gaussian probing）方法，通过测量LoRA适配器对模型内部表征的扰动来评估模型能力，无需生成任何输出，从而解决在法律受限领域（如CSAM检测）无法进行生成式评估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于输出的生成式评估（如红队测试）无法扩展至平台级审计，且在CSAM等法律约束领域中完全失效，因此需要一种无需生成输出的模型能力评估方法（Evaluation without Generation）。

Method: 提出高斯探针方法，通过测量模型对高斯潜变量集成的响应，量化LoRA适配器对预训练模型内部表征的扰动程度，从而区分良性微调与有害特化。

Result: 高斯探针在无需采样输出的条件下可靠地区分良性微调与有害特化，在CSAM等高危领域有效，且对权重缩放攻击具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 高斯探针为评估高危生成式系统提供了一种可扩展的非生成式替代方案，解决了平台级审计中输出受限场景下的安全治理难题。

Abstract: Auditing the fine-tunes of open-weight generative models for harmful specialization has become a new governance challenge for model hosting platforms. The standard toolkit, generative evaluation via curated prompts or red-teaming, does not scale to platform-level auditing and breaks down entirely for domains like CSAM where generation is legally constrained. This motivates the Evaluation without Generation problem: assessing model capabilities without producing outputs. We argue that in such settings, capability must be inferred from the model's state, either its parameters or internal representations, rather than its outputs. We introduce Gaussian probing, a method that characterizes how LoRA adaptors perturb a model's internal representations by measuring responses to Gaussian latent ensembles. Unlike raw-weight baselines, Gaussian probing reliably distinguishes benign from harmful specialization without sampling outputs. We demonstrate effectiveness in high-risk domains, including detecting models specialized for child sexual abuse material (CSAM), where output-based evaluation is legally and ethically constrained. Our results show that Gaussian probing provides a scalable non-generative alternative for evaluating high-risk generative systems and remains robust to weight rescaling, a representative adversarial manipulation.

</details>


### [147] [Towards Unified Multi-task EEG Analysis with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25131)
*Sicheng Dai,Kai Chen,Hongwang Xiao,Shan Yu,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MTEEG框架，通过任务特定的低秩适配（LoRA）模块实现多任务脑电图（EEG）分析，在解耦参数空间缓解任务冲突的同时，在六个下游任务上超越单任务SOTA方法，推动通用脑机接口发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
